12:Tf71,window.dataLayer=window.dataLayer||[];
function gtag(){dataLayer.push(arguments);}
/*
 * Two defaults, most specific region wins (see the file header). Both carry
 * `wait_for_update`, which holds the first ping back so it can carry a CMP's
 * answer when there is one; with no CMP no update ever arrives, gtag proceeds
 * after 2s under the regional default, and the first hit is delayed by that
 * much for everyone — the same latency the stub grace path costs.
 */
gtag('consent','default',{
 ad_storage:'denied',
 ad_user_data:'denied',
 ad_personalization:'denied',
 analytics_storage:'denied',
 wait_for_update:2000,
 region:["AT","BE","BG","HR","CY","CZ","DK","EE","FI","FR","DE","GR","HU","IS","IE","IT","LV","LI","LT","LU","MT","NL","NO","PL","PT","RO","SK","SI","ES","SE","GB","CH"]
});
gtag('consent','default',{
 ad_storage:'granted',
 ad_user_data:'granted',
 ad_personalization:'granted',
 analytics_storage:'granted',
 wait_for_update:2000
});
gtag('js',new Date());
gtag('config','G-Y71SJTDC68');
(function(){
 var tries=0;
 var settled=false;
 var provisional=null;
 var graceTimer=null;

 /*
  * TCF gives us purpose and vendor consents; Google Analytics is NOT a TCF
  * vendor, so the mapping below is ours to make and to defend:
  *   purpose 1    — store/access information on a device. This is the ePrivacy
  *                  gate for writing ANY cookie, so every grant depends on it.
  *   purpose 8    — measure content performance. That is what GA4 does here.
  *   vendor 755   — Google Advertising Products, which is what the ad_* signals
  *                  actually govern.
  *   purposes 3+4 — build/use profiles to select personalised ads.
  * It is a mapping over TCF, not over any one vendor, so it survives a change
  * of ad network unchanged.
  */
 function verdict(d){
  if(d.gdprApplies===false){
   return {
    ad_storage:'granted',
    ad_user_data:'granted',
    ad_personalization:'granted',
    analytics_storage:'granted'
   };
  }
  var p=(d.purpose&&d.purpose.consents)||{};
  var v=(d.vendor&&d.vendor.consents)||{};
  var storage=!!p[1];
  var google=!!v[755];
  return {
   analytics_storage:storage&&!!p[8]?'granted':'denied',
   ad_storage:storage&&google?'granted':'denied',
   ad_user_data:storage&&google?'granted':'denied',
   ad_personalization:storage&&google&&!!p[3]&&!!p[4]?'granted':'denied'
  };
 }

 /*
  * A stub answer. See the file header: these three statuses mean "the CMP has
  * not decided yet", and taking their gdprApplies at face value is how an EEA
  * reader gets consented without being asked.
  */
 function isProvisional(d){
  var s=d&&d.cmpStatus;
  return s==='stub'||s==='loading'||s==='disabled';
 }

 function settle(d){
  if(settled){return;}
  settled=true;
  if(graceTimer){clearTimeout(graceTimer);graceTimer=null;}
  gtag('consent','update',verdict(d));
 }

 function startGrace(){
  if(graceTimer||settled){return;}
  graceTimer=setTimeout(function(){
   graceTimer=null;
   if(settled||!provisional){return;}
   settled=true;
   gtag('consent','update',verdict(provisional));
  },1500);
 }

 function apply(d){
  if(!d||settled){return;}
  if(isProvisional(d)){provisional=d;startGrace();return;}
  settle(d);
 }

 function attach(){
  if(typeof window.__tcfapi==='function'){
   window.__tcfapi('addEventListener',2,function(d,ok){
    if(ok&&d&&(d.eventStatus==='tcloaded'||d.eventStatus==='useractioncomplete')){apply(d);}
   });
   return;
  }
  /*
   * ~6s of polling because a CMP may define __tcfapi from an async script.
   * If it never shows up — no provider active, which is the state today, or
   * the CMP blocked by an ad blocker — we push NO update and the regional
   * default stands: denied in the EEA, the UK and Switzerland, granted
   * elsewhere. Absence of a consent authority is not consent, and the region
   * default is what encodes that.
   */
  if(++tries>60){return;}
  setTimeout(attach,100);
 }

 attach();
